*Uḥilla ṣaydu'l-baḥri... wa ḥurrima ʿalaykum ṣaydu'l-barri mā dumtum ḥuruman* — sea-game lawful, land-game forbidden-in-iḥrām: the *baḥr-barr* (sea-land) distinction — and its wisdom rewards reflection. *Sea's latitude*: fish-and-seafood permitted (even in iḥrām — this verse; *sayyārah*-provisioned — travelers fed), sea-carrion lawful (*"its dead is lawful,"* al-Tirmidhī 69 — the sea's purity: *"its water purifying,"* abū Dāwūd 83 — necessity's latitude). *Land's restriction*: game forbidden-in-iḥrām (vv. 94–96), Ḥaram-game forbidden-always (sanctuary's peace — 27:91), Ḥaram-vegetation protected (cutting-barred). *The wisdom*: iḥrām's burden *bounded* (sea-proteins available — hardship mitigated), land's symbolism *preserved* (pilgrimage-terrain sacralized — violence renounced where rites performed), traveler's needs *secured* (*sayyārah* — provision universalized). *The fiqh*: sea-game defined (fish unanimous; sea-beasts debated — Ḥanafī-fish-only, majority-all-sea), land-game defined (edible-wild — penalty-laddered, v. 95). Liberate seas; restrict lands; feed travelers. Geography legislated; wisdom manifold.

**Tuḥsharūn: Gathering as Reverence's Ground**

*Wattaqū'Llāha'lladhī ilayhi tuḥsharūn* — "revere God, unto Whom you shall be gathered": *ḥashr* (gathering, resurrection-assembly) — reverence's *eschatological* ground — and the grounding needs its assembly-scene. *The gathering*: all assembled (18:47: *"gathered, omitting none"*; 6:22; 10:28), hunters-and-hunted (game witnessing — 6:38's communities-gathered; 81:5's beasts-assembled), pilgrims-and-violators (iḥrām-deeds reviewed). *The ground*: gathering-certain → reverence-rational (hunting-judged — v. 95's *wabāl* tasted fully; restraint-rewarded — v. 94's unseen-fear honored). *The Names*: *ilayhi* (unto-Him — gathering's destination Divine), *tuḥsharūn* (passive — gathered-unwilling-or-willing — 19:68's knees-dragged; 50:44's swift-emerging). *The verse's arc* (94–96): tried-by-game (94), priced-for-violation (95), provisioned-by-sea (96) — reverence *crowning* (gathering-grounded). Gathered certainly; judged comprehensively; revered presently. *Tuḥsharūn* — destination certain, reverence commanded, assembly awaiting.

---
## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:97

> **God has made the Kaʿbah, the Sacred House, a support for mankind, as well as the sacred month, the offering, and the garlands — that you may know that God knows what is in the heavens and what is in the earth, and that God is the Knower of all things.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Qiyām for Mankind: The House as Support**

*God made the Kaʿbah* (*Kaʿbah* — cube, elevation), *the Sacred House* (*al-Bayt al-Ḥarām*), *qiyāman li'n-nās* — support, standing, sustenance for mankind (cf. 4:5's wealth-*qiyām*; 3:96–97's first-House). *Qiyām* (standing, support, maintenance): the House *upholding* (pilgrimage-economies sustained — Makkan trade, global *Ḥajj*-industries; spiritual-economies anchored — prayers-oriented, hearts-gathered; temporal-economies ordered — sacred-months observed, truces honored). *Sacred month* (*shahr ḥarām* — Rajab, Dhū-Qaʿdah, Dhū-Ḥijjah, Muḥarram — fighting-barred, pilgrimage-secured), *offering* (*hady* — sacrificial animals — v. 2's *qalā'id*-garlanded), *garlands* (*qalā'id* — neck-markers designating sanctity — v. 2) — sanctity's *system* (House-centered, time-extended, offering-supplied). *That you may know God knows heavens-and-earth* — sanctity-system *evidencing* omniscience (legislation-wise, provision-ordered). House supporting; months securing; offerings feeding. *Qiyām* universal; knowledge total.

**Al-Bayt al-Ḥarām: The Sacred House's Offices**

*Al-Kaʿbata'l-Bayta'l-Ḥarām* — the Kaʿbah, Sacred House: its *offices* (functions) need inventorying — *qiyām* (support) specified. *Orientation-office*: *qiblah* (2:144–150 — prayers-facing, unity-embodied — 1.8-billion daily-orienting). *Pilgrimage-office*: *Ḥajj*-and-ʿUmrah (3:97; 22:27–29 — journeys-obligatory, sins-washed — *"returns as newborn,"* al-Bukhārī 1521). *Sanctuary-office*: *ḥaram*-safety (27:91's Lord-of-city; 29:67's secure-sanctuary — violence-barred, game-protected, vegetation-spared). *Economic-office*: *qiyām*-provision (Quraysh's winter-summer caravans — 106:1–4; modern *Ḥajj*-economies — millions sustained). *Covenantal-office*: Abrahamic-foundation (2:127's raising; 14:37's progeny-settled; 22:26's purification-commanded). *Eschatological-office*: gathering-symbol (resurrection's *ḥashr* prefigured — multitudes circling). *Qiyāman li'n-nās* (support-for-*mankind* — universal, not Muslim-only): sanctity *benefiting* all (peace-zones, charity-flows, orientation-given). House officing; mankind supported; knowledge evidenced.

**Li-Taʿlamū: Legislation Evidencing Omniscience**

*Dhālika li-taʿlamū anna'Llāha yaʿlamu mā fi's-samāwāti wa mā fi'l-arḍi wa anna'Llāha bi-kulli shay'in ʿalīm* — "that you may know God knows all": sanctity-legislation *evidencing* omniscience — the epistemology: wise-laws proving Knowing-Lawgiver. *The evidence*: House-placed (Makkah-centered — geography-optimal: trade-routes, water-Zamzam, mountain-sheltered), months-sacralized (fighting-paused — Arabia's war-cycle interrupted, pilgrimage-secured), offerings-ordained (protein-distributed — millions-fed, charity-systemized), garlands-marked (sanctity-signaled — violence-deterred). *The inference*: legislation-this-wise → Legislator-all-knowing (needs-anticipated, harms-prevented, benefits-maximized). *Bi-kulli shay'in ʿalīm* (Knower-of-*all*): omniscience *total* (heavens-earth, seen-unseen, past-future — 6:59's keys-of-unseen; 34:3's atom-unhidden). Know thereby: laws evidencing, wisdom inferring, omniscience certain. *Li-taʿlamū* — legislation teaching theology. House legislated; knowledge proven; Knower known.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:98

> **Know that God is severe in retribution, and that God is Forgiving, Merciful.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Severe and Forgiving: The Twin Knowledge**

*Know* (*iʿlamū* — imperative: knowledge-commanded) *that God is severe in retribution* (*shadīd al-ʿiqāb*) *and God is Forgiving, Merciful* (*Ghafūr Raḥīm*) — the twin-knowledge: severity-and-mercy *co-commanded* (know *both*, hold *both*, hope-fear *both*). *Shadīd al-ʿiqāb* (severe-in-retribution): punishment *certain* (violations requited — vv. 33, 38, 95's penalties; 37's lasting-torment), *proportionate* (deeds-matched — 99:7–8's atoms), *irresistible* (Might-executing — v. 95's *ʿAzīz*). *Ghafūr Raḥīm*: forgiveness *available* (repentance-accepted — vv. 34, 39, 74; 39:53), mercy *encompassing* (7:156's all-embracing). The pairing recurs (8:25; 40:3: *severe-punishing, long-bountying*; 41:43) — revelation's emotional *calibration*: fear-without-despair (severity-bounded-by-mercy), hope-without-presumption (mercy-bounded-by-severity). Know doubly; feel doubly; live balanced. Twin commanded; calibration achieved.

**Iʿlamū: Knowledge as Command**

*Iʿlamū anna...* — "know that...": *ʿilm* (knowledge) *commanded* (imperative) — theology as *duty* — and commanded-knowledge needs its epistemology. *Knowledge-commanded*: God's severity-mercy (this verse), God's sufficiency (9:104's repentance-accepting), lawful-unlawful (legislation-clarified — v. 89's *yubayyin*), provision-guaranteed (11:6). *Knowledge's fruits*: fear (35:28: *"only the knowledgeable fear God"* — severity known → reverence felt), hope (39:53 known → despair banned), worship (knowledge motivating — 51:56's worship-purpose). *Knowledge's sources*: revelation (this verse — severity-mercy *told*), reflection (creation's signs — 3:190–191), experience (consequences witnessed — v. 48's vindications). *Ignorance's perils*: presumption (mercy-only known — severity ignored), despair (severity-only known — mercy ignored), heedlessness (neither known — 7:179's cattle-worse). *Iʿlamū* — know! (imperative plural — community-commanded, perpetually). Know doubly (severe-forgiving); feel doubly (fear-hope); worship singly (the Known). Commanded knowledge; commanded balance; commanded life.

**Shadīd-Ghafūr: The Fear-Hope Calibration**

*Shadīdu'l-ʿiqābi... Ghafūrun Raḥīm* — Severe-in-retribution, Forgiving-Merciful: the *calibration-verse* — fear-and-hope *balanced* — and the balance needs its fulcrum (the Prophet's ﷺ dying-youth ḥadīth — v. 40's exposition: hope-and-fear combined → hope-granted, fear-secured). *Fear's function*: restraining (severity known → violations avoided — *taqwā*'s shield), sin-checking (punishment-certain → appetites curbed). *Hope's function*: sustaining (forgiveness-available → repentance attempted — despair banned, 39:53), worship-energizing (mercy-hoped → devotion offered). *Imbalance's diseases*: fear-only (despair — *ya's*, *qunūṭ* — 12:87; 39:53's prohibition), hope-only (presumption — *ghurūr*, *amn* — 7:99's plot-secured; Satan's lulling). *The fulcrum*: preponderance-debated (fear-exceeding in health — sins-restrained; hope-exceeding at death — *"die hoping-good of God,"* Muslim 2877 — meeting optimized). Know severely (fear), know forgivingly (hope), die hoping (meeting). Calibrated doubly; fulcrum wisely; meeting hopefully.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:99

> **Upon the Messenger is only the conveyance. And God knows what you reveal and what you conceal.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Only the Conveyance: The Messenger's Delimited Duty**

*Upon the Messenger is only conveyance* (*balāgh* — cf. vv. 67, 92; 16:35, 82; 24:54; 29:18; 64:12) — duty *delimited* (conveying, not compelling/converting/compelling-results). *God knows what you reveal* (*tubdūn* — manifesting) *and conceal* (*taktumūn* — hiding — cf. v. 61's *yaktumūn*) — omniscience *auditing* (professions weighed, concealments exposed). The pairing liberates-and-binds: Messenger liberated (results un-owned — vv. 41, 64, 68's consolations), hearers bound (responses recorded — revelation-and-concealment both known). *Mā ʿala'r-Rasūli illa'l-balāgh* (nothing-upon-Messenger but-conveyance): exclusion-*illā* (duty *only*-conveying), comfort-complete (rejection un-owned). Convey solely; know doubly (revealed-concealed); audit totally. Delimited duty; total knowledge; perfect justice.

**Tubdūn wa Taktumūn: Revealed and Concealed Audited**

*Wa'Llāhu yaʿlamu mā tubdūna wa mā taktumūn* — "God knows revealed-and-concealed": omniscience *auditing* responses — and the audit's comprehensiveness needs its inventory. *Revealed* (*tubdūn* — manifesting): professions (belief-claimed — v. 61's *"we believe"*), performances (worship-displayed — 4:142's hypocrite-prayers), alliances (confederacies-public — vv. 51–52). *Concealed* (*taktumūn* — hiding): hostilities (v. 61's disbelief-hidden), mockery (v. 57–58's ridicule-private), hedges (v. 52's insurance-secret). *The knowledge*: total (50:16's nearer-than-vein; 67:13–14's secret-and-open; 20:7's secret-and-more-hidden). *The audit's function*: hypocrisy-exposed (v. 61's commuters-known), sincerity-honored (hidden-charity rewarded — 2:271's secretly-given), justice-perfected (verdicts-informed — 22:17's judging-between). Reveal-or-conceal: known. Profess-or-hide: audited. *Tubdūn-taktumūn* — both inventoried, all judged, justice total.

**Balāgh's Sufficiency: Results Relinquished**

*Mā ʿala'r-Rasūli illa'l-balāghu* — Messenger's duty *only*-conveyance: results *relinquished* — and the relinquishment's liberation needs its charter (warners' burnout prevented). *Relinquished*: conversions (hearts God's — 28:56), compliance (choices hearers' — 18:29's believe-or-disbelieve), outcomes (victories God's — 8:17's throwing; 110:1's help-coming). *Retained*: clarity (conveyance *mubīn* — manifest — v. 92), completeness (conveyance *total* — v. 67's *balligh-all*), compassion (conveyance *gentle* — 20:44; 3:159). *The charter*: deliver-fully (responsibility-total), release-outcomes (anxiety-zero), trust-God (results-His — vv. 41, 64, 68). *The audit-comfort*: God-knowing-revealed-concealed (responses recorded) *validates* conveyance (efforts witnessed — 9:105's God-seeing; 53:39–41's striving-seen-rewarded). Convey completely; relinquish results; witnessed thereby. Sufficiency liberating; audit comforting; charter perpetual.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:100

> **Say, "The evil and the good are not equal, though the abundance of evil may please thee." So reverence God, O possessors of intellect, that haply you may prosper.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Evil and Good Unequal: Quality Over Quantity**

*The evil* (*khabīth* — foul, impure) *and good* (*ṭayyib* — pure, wholesome) *are not equal* (*lā yastawī*) — *though evil's abundance* (*kathrah* — multitude) *pleases* (*yuʿjib* — impresses, delights) — quality-over-quantity canonized: multitudes never validating (cf. 6:116: *most-on-earth mislead*; v. 81's *many-fāsiqūn*; 12:103). *Khabīth-ṭayyib*: disbelief-and-belief (8:37's sifting), unlawful-and-lawful (wealth — *suḥt*-abundant versus ḥalāl-scarce), hypocrites-and-sincere (multitudes versus remnants). *Abundance-pleasing*: majorities impressing (crowds suggesting truth), wealth dazzling (*khabīth*-fortunes envied), power awing (tyrannies admired) — *yuʿjibka* (pleasing-*thee* — even prophets tempted-by-appearances — human susceptibility acknowledged). *So revere God, O possessors-of-intellect* (*ulū'l-albāb* — cf. 2:179; 3:7, 190; 39:9) — *ʿaql* (intellect) *commanding* reverence (reason concluding: evil-unequal → God-revered). *That you may prosper* (*tufliḥūn* — *falāḥ*'s harvest — vv. 35, 90). Unequal declared; abundance discounted; intellect summoned.

**Kathrat al-Khabīth: Abundance's Seduction**

*Wa law aʿjabaka kathratu'l-khabīth* — "though evil's abundance impresses thee": *kathrah* (multitude) *seducing* — and abundance-seduction needs its casebook (majorities misleading). *The seductions*: numerical (crowds — 6:116's earth-majority misguiding; Noah's few-versus-multitudes — 11:40's *qalīl*), material (wealth-abundant — Qārūn's treasures dazzling — 28:79–80; *khabīth*-fortunes envied), institutional (powers-mighty — Pharaoh's hosts awing — 26:53–59; empires enduring). *The address* (*aʿjabaka* — impressing-*thee*, singular — Prophet ﷺ addressed, susceptibility humanized): even prophets *tempted* by appearances (multitudes suggesting validity), revelation *correcting* (quality-criteria). *The criteria*: *istiwā'*-denied (unequal — *lā yastawī*: 39:9's knowing-unknowing; 59:20's Garden-Fire; 35:19–22's blind-seeing, darkness-light) — goodness *intrinsic* (ṭayyib-valuable-scarce), evil *intrinsic* (khabīth-worthless-abundant). *La-... but revere* (intellect-commanded): *ulū'l-albāb* (possessors-of-kernels — intellects *essential*-grasping) revering (reason concluding worship). Discount multitudes; discern qualities; revere accordingly. Abundance seducing; intellect resisting; prosperity following.

**Ulū'l-Albāb: Intellects Summoned to Reverence**

*Fattaqū'Llāha yā ulī'l-albāb* — "revere God, O possessors-of-intellect": *ulū'l-albāb* (kernel-possessors — *lubb*: kernel, essence, pure intellect) — summoned to *taqwā* — reason concluding worship. *The ulū'l-albāb-portrait*: reflecting (3:190–191: heavens-earth pondered — *"Lord, not in vain"*), remembering (13:19–22: covenant-keeping, patience, charity-secret-open), discerning (39:9: knowing-versus-unknowing distinguished; 39:17–18: best-speech followed), heeding (14:52: warned-benefiting; 38:29: blessed-reflecting). *The summons*: reverence *intellectual* (reason-demanded — evil-unequal → God-revered), prosperity *intellectual* (*laʿallakum tufliḥūn* — intellects prospering — *falāḥ*'s harvest reaped-by-reason). *The contrast*: *lā yaʿqilūn* (understanding-not — v. 58's mockers; 8:22's worst-creatures) versus *ulū'l-albāb* (understanding-essentially) — intellect's presence-or-absence deciding destinies. Possess kernels; ponder essentially; revere rationally. Summoned intellects; promised prosperity; *falāḥ* reaped.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:101

> **O you who believe! Ask not about things which, if disclosed to you, would vex you. But if you ask about them when the Qur'an is being sent down, they will be disclosed to you. God has pardoned that. And God is Forgiving, Forbearing.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Ask Not What Vexes: The Question Moratorium**

*Ask not* (*lā tas'alū*) *about things which, disclosed, would vex* (*tasu'kum* — grieve, harm) — the question-moratorium: inquiry *bounded* (revelation-in-progress protected from vexatious questioning). The occasions: Companions asking hypothetically (Ḥajj-annual? — *"if I said yes, it would be obligated, and you could not"* — Muslim 1337), skeptically (lost-camel's whereabouts, paternity-challenges — al-Bukhārī 7294–7295's *aghdabūhu* — angering him), excessively (Bani-Isrā'īl-pattern — 2:67–71's cow-interrogated). *But if you ask when Qur'an descending, disclosed* — revelation *answering* (questions revealing-laws: 2:189's crescents; 2:219's wine; 8:1's spoils — sincere inquiry legislating). *God pardoned that* (*ʿafā* — past-excesses effaced) — mercy-for-curiosity-past. *Forgiving, Forbearing* (*Ghafūr Ḥalīm* — forbearance *delaying*-punishment — curiosity-indulged). Ask wisely (sincere-beneficial), refrain wisely (vexatious-hypothetical), pardoned graciously. Moratorium bounded; revelation answering; forbearance covering.

**Lā Tas'alū: Question-Ethics Legislated**

*Lā tas'alū ʿan ashyā'a in tubda lakum tasu'kum* — "ask not things vexing-if-disclosed": question-*ethics* legislated — inquiry *disciplined* — and the discipline needs its manual. *Questions-barred*: vexatious (disclosure-harming — paternity-doubts, hypothetically-burdening — Ḥajj-annual?), mocking (skeptics-testing — camel-whereabouts, Hour-demanding — 33:63's timing-pressed), excessive (cow-interrogating — 2:67–71's specification-multiplying — ease-complicated). *Questions-welcomed*: sincere (need-driven — crescents, menses, spoils — revelation-answering), beneficial (practice-guiding — lawful-unlawful sought), timely (revelation-descending — disclosure-seasonal). *The Prophet's ﷺ rule*: *"leave me with what I leave you — those-before perished by much-questioning and prophet-disputing"* (Muslim 1337); *"greatest sin: asking about unprohibited, then prohibited for his asking"* (al-Bukhārī 7289; Muslim 2358 — hypotheticals legislating-burdens). *ʿAfā'Llāhu ʿanhā* (God pardoned-them): past-questioning effaced (curiosity-indulged, vexation-forgiven). Ask sincerely-beneficially-timely; refrain vexatiously-mockingly-excessively. Ethics legislated; manual issued; forbearance sealing.

**Ghafūr-Ḥalīm: Forgiveness with Forbearance**

*Wa'Llāhu Ghafūrun Ḥalīm* — "Forgiving, Forbearing": *Ḥilm* (forbearance — anger-restrained, punishment-delayed) *paired* with forgiveness — curiosity's indulgence theologized. *Ḥalīm* (Forbearing): punishment-delaying (violations un-hastened — 11:114's good-effacing-evil awaited; 35:45's postponing), anger-restraining (provocations un-avenged — mockery endured — v. 57–58; questioning-excess tolerated — this verse), opportunity-granting (repentance-awaited — 39:53's despair-banned). *Ghafūr-Ḥalīm* (paired — 2:225; 3:155; 4:12; 5:101; 17:44; 35:41): forgiveness *with* forbearance (sins pardoned *patiently* — effacement unhurried, relapses tolerated-then-covered). *The verse's forbearance*: question-excesses pardoned (*ʿafā* — past effaced), revelation continuing (disclosure-seasonal — asking-answered-when-descending), curiosity indulged (learning permitted — teaching-patient). Thank forbearance (violations un-punished-swiftly), seek forgiveness (sins covered-fully), ask disciplinedly (ethics honored). Forbearing indulging; Forgiving covering; questions disciplined.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:102

> **A people before you asked about them, then they came to disbelieve therein.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Asked, Then Disbelieved: The Precedent's Warning**

*A people before you asked* (*sa'alahā qawmun min qablikum*) *about them* (such things — revelations-details, prophet-proofs, hypothetical-legislations), *then they disbelieved therein* (*aṣbaḥū bihā kāfirīn* — becoming disbelievers *about them*) — the precedent warning: questioning-patterns *damning* (inquiry-motivated-by-defiance producing disbelief). The candidates: Thamūd asking-then-hamstringing (she-camel sign-demanded — 7:73; 17:59 — then slaughtered), Israel asking-then-worshipping (calf *after* signs — 2:51–54; cow-specifications *then* reluctance — 2:67–71), Quraysh asking-then-denying (signs-demanded — 17:90–93; 6:37 — belief-withheld). *Aṣbaḥū* (becoming, morning-after): questioning's *morning* (answers-received, signs-shown) dawning disbelief (gratitude-absent, submission-withheld). The verse closes the question-moratorium (vv. 101–102): ask-not (101), precedent-cited (102) — curiosity's cautionary-tale. Asked excessively; answered clearly; disbelieved consequently. Precedent warning; moratorium sealed.

**Sa'alū fa-Kafarū: Questioning That Damns**

*Qad sa'alahā qawmun min qablikum thumma aṣbaḥū bihā kāfirīn* — "a people asked, then became disbelieving": questioning *damning* — inquiry-to-disbelief pipeline — and the pipeline's stages need mapping (question-ethics' horror-cases). *Stage one* (sign-demanding): Thamūd's she-camel (7:73 — sign-specified), Israel's calf-time revelations (2:51 — Moses-absent exploited), Quraysh's treasure-angel garden demands (17:90–93). *Stage two* (sign-receiving): she-camel-emerging (7:73 — proof-granted), tablets-revealed (7:145 — guidance-inscribed), Qur'an-descending (17:88's inimitability — sign-supreme). *Stage three* (sign-defying): she-camel-hamstrung (7:77 — proof-slaughtered), calf-worshipped (2:51 — guidance-abandoned), Qur'an-called-sorcery (74:24 — supreme-denied). *Aṣbaḥū bihā kāfirīn* (morning-after disbelieving): answers *received*, disbelief *chosen* — knowledge-aggravating (proofs witnessed, denial deliberate — 27:14's certainty-with-injustice). *The pipeline's lesson*: ask-sincerely (submission-intended) or refrain (defiance-masked-inquiry damning). Questioned defiantly; answered clearly; damned deliberately. Pipeline mapped; horror displayed; sincerity demanded.

**Thumma: The "Then" of Tragedy**

*Thumma aṣbaḥū bihā kāfirīn* — "*then* they became disbelieving": *thumma* (then — sequential, consequential) — tragedy's conjunction — and the *thumma*-tragedies across revelation need hearing. *Question-then-disbelief* (this verse): asked → answered → denied. *Sign-then-defiance*: she-camel → hamstrung (7:77); sea-parted → calf-worshipped (2:50–51); manna-sent → complained (2:61). *Blessing-then-rebellion*: garden-given → poor-barred (68:17–24); kingdom-granted → tyranny-practiced (Pharaoh — 10:83); scripture-sent → distorted (2:75; 5:13, 41). *Thumma*'s delay (*thumma* implying interval — not instant): grace-periods (answers pondered, signs witnessed, blessings enjoyed) *then* tragedy (deliberation-time aggravating — considered-rejection worse than impulsive). *Aṣbaḥū* (morning-after): tragedy's *dawn* (night-of-answering ending, day-of-denying breaking). Mind the *thumma*: intervals grace-filled, tragedies chosen, mornings-after damning. Asked-answered (*thumma*) denied. Interval graced; choice damned; morning rued.

---
## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:103

> **God has ordained neither baḥīrah, nor sā'ibah, nor waṣīlah, nor ḥām. But those who disbelieve forge lies against God, and most of them understand not.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Four Pagan Cattle-Laws: Superstition Disowned**

*God ordained neither baḥīrah* (slit-eared she-camel dedicated to idols), *nor sā'ibah* (freed-roaming camel vowed for gods), *nor waṣīlah* (ewe twinning female-then-male, dedicated), *nor ḥām* (stallion exempted after siring-ten) — four pagan cattle-superstitions (Ṭ, W; cf. 6:136–140's dedications) *disowned*: *mā jaʿala'Llāh* (God-made-*nothing* — Divine disavowal absolute). *But disbelievers forge lies* (*yaftarūn al-kadhib* — inventing: *"God commanded these"* — 7:28's *"God commanded indecency"* parallel) *against God* — superstition *theologized* (customs Divinely-attributed), *most understanding-not* (*lā yaʿqilūn* — cf. v. 58's mockers; 8:22). The verse's method: pagan practices *named* (four specified — precision), Divinely *disowned* (authorship-denied), mendaciously *exposed* (forgery-charged), irrationally *diagnosed* (mindlessness-verdict). Name superstitions; disown Divinely; expose mendaciously; diagnose cognitively.

**Mā Jaʿala'Llāh: Divine Disavowal of Superstition**

*Mā jaʿala'Llāhu min baḥīratin...* — "God made nothing of...": *jaʿl*-denied (ordaining-negated) — Divine *disavowal* — and disavowal's scope needs extending (all superstitions disowned). *Disavowed*: cattle-dedications (this verse's four — pagan-Arabian), food-taboo inventions (6:138–140's grain-and-cattle restrictions — *"forbidden to women"*), Sabbath-burdens (Israelite stringencies — human-added), monasticism-invented (57:27's *rahbāniyyah* — *"We ordained it not"* — *mā katabnāhā*). *The forgery* (*iftirā' ʿala'Llāh* — lying-against-God): superstitions *Divinely-attributed* (*"God commanded"* — customs sanctified), prohibitions *invented* (16:116's *"this lawful, this forbidden"* — tongues legislating), worships *fabricated* (*bidʿah*'s theologizing — innovations Sunnah-claimed). *Iftirā*'s gravity: worst-lying (6:21, 93; 7:37 — *man aẓlamu*, who-more-wrong?), Fire-earning (39:32's liars-abiding), curse-drawing (3:61's *mubāhalah*). *Lā yaʿqilūn* (understanding-not): superstition *mindless* (reason-abandoned — customs-unexamined). Disown Divinely; expose forgery; diagnose mindlessness. *Mā jaʿala* — God ordained *nothing* thereof.

**Superstition's Afterlife: Modern Baḥīrahs**

The four cattle-laws died; superstition's *species* survives — modern *baḥīrahs* needing the verse's disavowal. *Lucky-objects*: charms-and-amulets (efficacy-attributed — *tamā'im* — *"whoever hangs, entrusted-to-it,"* Abū Dāwūd 3883), relic-veneration (efficacy-located — objects worship-adjacent). *Omen-systems*: horoscopes (decisions-starred — *azlām*'s modernity, v. 90), numerology (numbers-feared — 13-phobia, omens-counted), dream-oracles (unauthorized-interpretations binding). *Innovated-worships*: saint-vow dedications (offerings-sainted — *nadhr*-misdirected), shrine-rituals (circumambulations-graved — ṭawāf-counterfeited), calendar-superstitions (Ṣafar-ill-omened — *"no Ṣafar,"* al-Bukhārī 5707; Muslim 2220). *The verse's test* (*mā jaʿala'Llāh* — did God ordain?): practices revelation-unfounded (Qur'an-Sunnah silent), Divinely-unattributable (forgery-if-attributed), reason-unsupported (*lā yaʿqilūn* — mindlessness). Apply test; disown superstitions; worship revelation-only. Modernity's *baḥīrahs* named; Divine disavowal extended; *iftirā'* exposed.

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:104

> **And when it is said unto them, "Come unto what God has sent down, and unto the Messenger," they say, "Sufficient for us is that wherein we found our fathers." What, even though their fathers understood nothing, nor were they guided?**

**Expanded Commentary**

**"Sufficient Is Our Fathers": Tradition's Last Refuge**

*Come to what God sent down, and to the Messenger* (*taʿālaw* — invitation-issued) — *they say: sufficient* (*ḥasbunā*) *is our fathers'* (*mā wajadnā ʿalayhi ābā'anā* — forefather-custom) — tradition's last-refuge (argument-exhausted, custom-invoked). *What, even though fathers understood nothing* (*lā yaʿlamūna*) *nor were guided* (*lā yahtadūn*)? — the refutation: forefathers *unknowing* (ignorance-transmitting), *unguided* (error-perpetuating) — sufficiency-claims voided. The verse parallels 2:170 (*"though fathers understood nothing?"*) — revelation's standing tradition-critique (custom tested-by-truth, forefathers weighed-by-guidance). *Ḥasbunā* (sufficient-for-us): sufficiency *misplaced* (forefathers sufficing — revelation redundant-claimed), *taʿālaw* (come!): invitation *outstanding* (revelation summoning — custom transcended). Come revelation-ward; forefathers weighed; sufficiency relocated.

**Ḥasbunā Mā Wajadnā: Sufficiency Misplaced**

*Qālū ḥasbunā mā wajadnā ʿalayhi ābā'anā* — "sufficient is our fathers' way": *ḥasb* (sufficiency) *misplaced* — forefathers sufficing — and misplaced-sufficiency recurs across denials. *The claim's logic*: antiquity-validating (old-true), majority-confirming (fathers-many), comfort-preferring (custom-easy) — revelation *redundant* (innovation-suspected), Messenger *unnecessary* (fathers-sufficient). *The refutation's logic* (*a-wa-law kāna...* — what-even-if?): fathers *unknowing* (*lā yaʿlamūna* — ignorance: superstitions believed — v. 103's cattle-laws; idols worshipped), fathers *unguided* (*lā yahtadūn* — error: paths strayed — v. 77's triple-straying; guidance-absent). *Qur'anic parallels*: 2:170 (forefathers-mindless), 7:28 (*"we found fathers doing it"* — indecency-traditioned), 43:22–24 (*"we found fathers on a path"* — messengers-countering: *"though I bring better-guidance?"*), 26:74 (*"we found fathers worshipping"* — Abraham-countering). *The criterion*: forefathers *tested* (knowing? guided?) — honored-if-righteous (Abraham's *millah* — 2:135; 16:123), abandoned-if-erring (this verse's unknowing-unguided). Sufficiency God's (39:36: *a-laysa'Llāhu bi-kāfin* — God-sufficing), guidance revelation's, forefathers tested. Misplacement corrected; invitation standing; come!

**Tradition Tested: The Qur'an's Custom-Criterion**

*A-wa-law kāna ābā'uhum lā yaʿlamūna shay'an wa lā yahtadūn* — "though fathers knew nothing, guided-nowhere?": tradition *tested* — custom-criterion legislated — and the criterion's applications need systematizing (Muslim traditionalisms audited). *The criterion*: customs revelation-tested (*muḥaymin*-criterion — v. 48: confirming-genuine, correcting-corrupt), forefathers guidance-weighed (knowing-guided honored; unknowing-unguided abandoned). *Tested-and-kept*: Abrahamic-rites (Ḥajj — 22:26–27; circumcision — *fitrah* — al-Bukhārī 5891), Arab-virtues (hospitality, courage — Islam-confirming), cultural-neutrals (dress, cuisine, languages — *mubāḥ*-preserved). *Tested-and-abandoned*: pagan-rites (cattle-laws — v. 103; idol-rites — 7:28), jāhiliyyah-customs (female-infanticide — 16:58–59; 81:8–9; blood-feuds — Farewell-Sermon's abolition), innovated-worships (*bidʿah* — *"every innovation misguidance,"* Muslim 867). *The method*: *taʿālaw* (come-to-revelation — invitation-first), *ḥasbunā*-tested (sufficiency-claims weighed), forefather-audited (knowing-guided?). Test traditions; keep righteous; abandon erring. Criterion legislated; customs sifted; revelation supreme.

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:105

> **O you who believe! You are responsible for your own souls. Those who go astray cannot harm you if you are rightly guided. Unto God shall be your return all together, and He will inform you of what you used to do.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Your Souls Are Your Responsibility: Guidance's Individuality**

*You are responsible for your own souls* (*ʿalaykum anfusakum* — upon-you your-souls: self-charge) — *strayers cannot harm you if rightly-guided* (*ih-tadaytum* — guidance-achieved) — responsibility *individualized* (souls owned-personally), strayers *neutralized* (astray-others harming-not — 6:164; 17:15; 35:18; 39:7's burden-unborne). The verse's occasion-context (post-tradition-critique — v. 104): believers *released* from forefathers'-errors (fathers straying harming-not — guidance personal), *charged* with self-rectitude (souls owned — excuses voided). *Unto God return-together, informed of deeds* (*fa-yunabbi'ukum* — informing — cf. v. 48's *fa-yunabbi'ukum bi-mā ta-khtalifūn*) — eschatological audit (returns universal, information complete). Own souls; strayers neutralized; audit certain. Individuality charged; community released-appropriately; God informing.

**ʿAlaykum Anfusakum: Self-Charge Versus Quietism**

*ʿAlaykum anfusakum lā yaḍurrukum man ḍalla idha'htadaytum* — "your souls upon-you; strayers harm-not if guided": self-*charge* — and the charge's quietism-misreading needs refuting (Abū Bakr's famous sermon correcting: *"you recite this verse, misplacing it — I heard the Messenger: when people see wrong un-forbidden, punishment nears-all"* — Abū Dāwūd 4338; al-Tirmidhī 2168; Ibn Mājah 4005). *The charge*: self-rectitude *primary* (souls reformed-first — guidance embodied), others'-straying *unharming* (guided unharmed — strayers' sins un-transferred — 6:164). *The misreading*: quietism (forbidding-abandoned — *"my soul suffices"* — v. 79's *lā yatanāhawna* repeated), isolationism (community-deserted — counsel-withheld). *The correction* (Abū Bakr): *ihtidā'* (right-guidance) *includes* forbidding-wrong (guidance-complete — tongue-level — Muslim 49 — practiced), self-charge *encompassing* mutual-correction (souls saved-together — ship-parable — Bukhārī 2493). *Idha'htadaytum* (if *truly*-guided): guidance-total (self reformed *and* others counseled — 3:104, 110; 9:71). Charge self; counsel others; guided-truly. Quietism refuted; correction included; Abū Bakr clarifying.

**Fa-Yunabbi'ukum: The Informing Return**

*Ilā'Llāhi marjiʿukum jamīʿan fa-yunabbi'ukum bi-mā kuntum taʿmalūn* — "return-together, informed of deeds": the *informing*-return — eschatological audit's *pedagogy* (informed, not merely sentenced). *The return*: *marjiʿ* (return-place — cf. v. 48's *marjiʿukum*), *jamīʿan* (all-together — guided-and-strayed assembled — 18:47's none-omitted). *The informing* (*tanbī'ah* — informing, apprising): deeds *displayed* (records opened — 17:13–14's neck-suspended; 18:49's book-presented), disputes *resolved* (v. 48's *differences-informed*), motives *exposed* (intentions-weighed — Bukhārī 1's *niyyāt*). *The pedagogy*: informing-before-sentencing (verdicts understood — justice-manifested — 17:15's messenger-before-punishment; 6:131's warners-before-destroying), knowledge-completing (deeds owned — excuses voided — 75:14–15's self-witnessing). Own souls now; informed then; return certain. Audit pedagogical; information complete; justice manifest.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:106

> **O you who believe! Let there be witnesses among you when death approaches one of you, at the time of bequeathing — two just men among you, or two others from another folk if you are journeying upon the earth and the affliction of death befalls you. Detain them both after prayer; if you doubt, let them swear by God, "We shall not sell it for any price, though he be a kinsman, nor shall we conceal testimony for God. Truly then we would be among the sinners."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Witnesses at Death's Approach: Bequest-Testimony Legislated**

*Witnesses among you at death-approaching, bequest-time* (*ḥīna'l-waṣiyyah* — bequeathing-moment): *two just men among you* (*dhawā ʿadlin minkum* — cf. v. 95's *ḥakamayn*) *or two others-from-another-folk* (*ākharāni min ghayrikum* — non-Muslims, per majority — travel-necessity) *if journeying, death-affliction befalling* (*muṣībat al-mawt* — death-as-affliction). The occasion: Tamīm al-Dārī's pre-Islam Christian journey (Budayl's death, bequest-entrusted, cup-concealed — Ṭ, W; al-Bukhārī 2780's *Badīl*-narrative) legislating travel-bequests. *Detain after prayer* (*taḥbisūnahumā min baʿdi'ṣ-ṣalāh* — ʿAṣr-prayer — solemnity-maximized), *swearing-if-doubted* (*in irtabtum* — oath-procedure: *"sell-not for price, kin-or-not, conceal-not God's-testimony, else sinners"*). Bequests witnessed; travelers provided; oaths solemnized. Death legislated; testimony guarded; justice traveled.

**Shahādat al-Waṣiyyah: Bequest-Testimony's Jurisprudence**

*Shahādatun baynakum idhā ḥaḍara aḥadakumu'l-mawtu ḥīna'l-waṣiyyah* — "testimony among-you at death-presencing, bequest-timing": bequest-*testimony* — and its jurisprudence needs detailing. *The witnesses*: two-just-Muslims preferred (*dhawā ʿadlin minkum* — uprightness-required — 2:282's testified-accepted), two-non-Muslims permitted-in-travel (*min ghayrikum* — necessity-gating — journey + Muslim-absence — majority-view; minority: Muslims-other-tribes). *The procedure*: post-prayer-detention (*taḥbis* — solemnity — ʿAṣr-after — gathering-maximized), doubt-triggered-oaths (*ir­tiyāb* — suspicion-gated — *"sell-not, conceal-not, else-sinners"* — self-imprecation). *The bequest* (*waṣiyyah*): third-maximum (*"third, and third much,"* al-Bukhārī 1295; Muslim 1628 — Saʿd's bequest-capped), non-heir beneficiaries (heirs Qur'an-apportioned — 4:11–12; *"no bequest for heir,"* Abū Dāwūd 2870), debt-first (bequests-after-debts — 4:11–12's *min baʿdi waṣiyyah aw dayn*). *Muṣībat al-mawt* (death-as-*affliction*): mortality's calamity (grief-legislated-around — witnesses comforting, bequests settling). Witness doubly; bequest thirdly; swear solemnly. Travel provided; death witnessed; justice done.

**Qasam al-Irtiyāb: The Suspicion-Oath**

*In irtabtum fa-yuqsimāni bi'Llāhi... lā nashtarī bihī thamanan wa-law kāna dhā qurbā wa-lā naktumu shahādata'Llāhi innā idhan la-mina'l-āthimīn* — "if doubting, both-swearing: sell-not for price, kin-or-not, conceal-not, else-sinners": the *suspicion-oath* (*qasam*) — solemnity-procedure — and its elements need parsing. *The trigger*: *irtiyāb* (doubt, suspicion — heirs-suspecting — concealment-alleged, Tamīm's-cup paradigmatic). *The timing*: *min baʿdi'ṣ-ṣalāh* (after-prayer — ʿAṣr — solemnity-temporal: worship-fresh, gathering-present, hearts-softened). *The oath-content*: price-refusing (*lā nashtarī* — testimony unsold — v. 44's *paltry-price* refused), kin-impartial (*wa-law dhā qurbā* — relatives un-favored — 4:135's kin-justice), concealment-abjuring (*lā naktumu* — testimony manifested — 2:283's concealment-cursed), self-imprecating (*la-mina'l-āthimīn* — sin-confessed-if-lying — oath's teeth). *Fa-...* (procedure-mandatory — detention-and-oath commanded). Doubt oath-gated; prayer solemnized; testimony priced-never. *Qasam* parsed; justice traveled; bequests guarded.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:107

> **But if it is discovered that these two are guilty of sin, then two others shall stand in their place from among those most concerned, and let them swear by God, "Our testimony is worthier than the testimony of these two, and we have not transgressed. Truly then we would be among the wrongdoers."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Discovered Sinning: The Counter-Oath Procedure**

*If discovered* (*ʿuthira* — found-out, exposed) *that both sinned* (*istahaqqā ithman* — guilt-earned — perjury, concealment, Tamīm's-cup exposed) — *two others standing-place* (*ākharāni yaqūmāni maqāmahumā* — substitutes-rising) *from most-concerned* (*al-awlayān bi'l-amr* — nearest-heirs, right-holders) — *swearing: "our-testimony worthier* (*aḥaqqu* — truer, more-deserving) *than theirs, transgressing-not* (*lam naʿtadi* — bounds-honored), *else wrongdoers* (*ẓālimīn* — self-imprecation)". The procedure: perjury-exposed → substitutes-sworn → testimony-superseded — justice *correcting* (false-oaths voided, true-claims vindicated). *Aḥaqqu* (worthier): heir-testimony *preferring* (interest-holding, knowledge-having — Tamīm's-cup recovered, Budayl's-heirs compensated). Discovered sinning; substitutes standing; worthier swearing. Perjury corrected; heirs vindicated; justice perfected.

**Istihqāq al-Ithm: Guilt's Discovery**

*Fa-in ʿuthira ʿalā annahumā'staḥaqqā ithman* — "if found both guilt-earning sin": *istihqāq* (deserving, earning — guilt-*merited*) *discovered* (*ʿuthira* — stumbled-upon, exposed) — perjury's unmasking — and the unmasking's providence needs noting (Tamīm's cup surfacing-in-Makkah — concealment Divinely-exposed). *The sin*: perjury (false-oaths — v. 106's oath-violated), concealment (cup-hidden — testimony-betrayed), theft (bequest-pilfered — trust-violated). *The discovery*: *ʿuthira* (passive — discovered-by-God — 40:19's eyes-treachery-known; 50:16's whisper-known), evidence-surfacing (cup-found, witnesses-coming, conscience-confessing — Tamīm converting-then-confessing — *sīrah*'s sequel). *The consequence*: substitution-procedure (this verse — counter-oaths), perjury-punished (oath-violation requited — 16:91–94's covenant-breaking; 5:89's expiations exceeded-by-perjury), testimony-voided (false-oaths nullified — justice-restarted). Sin earning; discovery exposing; substitution correcting. *ʿUthira* providentially; *istahaqqā* justly; procedure perfectly.

**Shahādat al-Awlā: The Worthier Testimony**

*Fa-yuqsimāni bi'Llāhi la-shahādatunā aḥaqqu min shahādatihimā wa-ma'ʿtadaynā innā idhan la-mina'ẓ-ẓālimīn* — "swearing: our-testimony worthier, un-transgressing, else-wrongdoers": the *worthier*-testimony (*aḥaqqu*) — heir-oaths superseding — and supersession's logic needs stating. *The swearers*: *al-awlayān* (worthiest-two — nearest-heirs — interest-holding, loss-bearing — standing-where (*maqāmahumā*) perjurers-stood). *The claim*: *aḥaqqu* (worthier — truer-testimony: knowledge-direct — Budayl's-cup known; interest-aligned — heirs truth-seeking; oath-fresh — perjury-untainted). *The restraint*: *mā'ʿtadaynā* (transgressing-not — claims-bounded — cup-only, excess-refused — justice-measured). *The imprecation*: *la-mina'ẓ-ẓālimīn* (wrongdoers-if-lying — self-cursing — oath's teeth-sharpened). *Dhālika adnā...* (v. 108's purpose — testimony-truer, oath-feared). Worthier sworn; perjury superseded; heirs vindicated. *Aḥaqqu* claiming; *ʿudwān* refusing; *ẓulm* self-imprecating.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:108

> **That is more likely to ensure that testimony will be given in its true form, or that they will fear lest oaths be rebutted by other oaths after theirs. And reverence God and hearken. And God guides not iniquitous people.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Testimony Truer, Oaths Feared: The Procedure's Purpose**

*That* (counter-oath procedure — vv. 106–107) *is likelier* (*adnā* — nearer, more-likely) *for testimony-true-formed* (*an ya'tū bi'sh-shahādati ʿalā wajhihā* — testimony-upon-its-face — truthfully) *or oath-rebuttal feared* (*an turadda aymānun baʿda aymānihim* — oaths-returned after-oaths — perjury-deterred) — the legislation's *purpose* stated: truth SECURING (testimony trued) or deterrence WORKING (rebuttal feared). *Reverence God and hearken* (*ittaqū'Llāha wa'smaʿū* — taqwā-and-obedience — legislation-sealed) — *God guides not iniquitous* (*fāsiqīn* — persistently-departing — cf. vv. 47, 49, 59, 81) — guidance-withheld (perjurers un-guided). The verse legislates *transparency*: procedures truth-conducing (solemnity-structures), deterrents perjury-discouraging (rebuttal-threats), reverence commanding (hearing-obeyed). Truer testified; rebuttal feared; reverence commanded. Purpose stated; procedure perfected; legislation sealed.

**Adnā li'ṣ-Ṣidq: Likelier Unto Truth**

*Dhālika adnā an ya'tū bi'sh-shahādati ʿalā wajhihā* — "likelier testimony-true-formed": *adnā* (nearer, likelier) — legislation *truth-conducing* — and truth-conduciveness needs its design-theory (procedures engineered-for-veracity). *The engineering*: solemnity (post-prayer-oaths — v. 106 — hearts-softened), publicity (detention-before-community — shame-guarding), imprecation (self-cursing — teeth-bared), rebuttal-threat (counter-oaths — v. 107 — perjury-risked). *ʿAlā wajhihā* (upon-its-face — true-formed: testimony *facially*-true — manifest-veracious, undistorted). *The alternative* (*aw yakhāfū...* — or fearing): deterrence-backup (truth unloved → rebuttal feared — perjury uneconomic: false-oaths voided, true-claims vindicated, perjurers shamed). *Legislative-wisdom*: procedures dual-purposed (virtue-conducing *and* vice-deterring — truer-testimony *or* feared-rebuttal — either-achieving justice). Engineer truthward; deter perjury; achieve justice. *Adnā* designing; *wajh* facing; truth testifying.

**Ittaqū wa'smaʿū: Reverence and Hearkening Sealing**

*Wattaqū'Llāha wa'smaʿū wa'Llāhu lā yahdī'l-qawma'l-fāsiqīn* — "revere God, hearken — God guides-not iniquitous": the bequest-legislation (vv. 106–108) *sealed* — taqwā-and-hearing commanded, guidance-withheld warned. *Ittaqū* (revere): perjury-feared (God-watched — 50:16; oaths Divine-witnessed — 16:91), testimony-guarded (truth-sacred — 2:283). *Ismaʿū* (hearken — hear-and-obey: legislation-received — procedures-followed, commands-implemented). *Lā yahdī'l-fāsiqīn* (guiding-not iniquitous): perjurers-persisting unguided (truth-withheld — hearts-sealed — 2:7; outcomes-ruined — perjury-exposed — v. 107's *ʿuthira*). The seal's universality: *all* testimony legislation (2:282's debts; 24:4's accusations; 65:2's divorces — reverence-and-hearing sealing-each). Revere perpetually; hearken obediently; guided thereby. Seal sealing; legislation perfected; bequests guarded.

---
## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:109

> **On the Day when God gathers the messengers and says, "What was the response given to you?" They will say, "We have no knowledge. Truly it is Thou Who art the Knower of things unseen."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**"What Was Your Response?": Messengers Questioned**

*On the Day God gathers messengers* (*yajmaʿu* — assembling — 77:11's appointed-time; 4:41's witness-summoning) *saying: what responded-to-you* (*mādhā ujibtum* — response-received: belief-or-denial, obedience-or-defiance)? *They say: no-knowledge for-us* (*lā ʿilma lanā* — disclaimed: responses' realities unknown — hearts'-secrets hidden, hypocrisies-masked, outward-professions deceiving) — *Thou Knowest-unseen* (*ʿAllāmu'l-ghuyūb* — Knower-of-unseens — cf. vv. 116–117's Jesus-disclaiming). The scene: messengers *witnessing* (delivery-testified — *"have I conveyed?"* — Farewell Sermon's witnessing), *disclaiming* (response-realities God's — professions weighed-by-Omniscient), *deferring* (judgment God's — 22:17's judging-between). *Mādhā ujibtum* (what-answered?): missions audited (Noah's 950-years — 29:14; Ṣāliḥ's nine-conspirators — 27:48–49; Muḥammad's ﷺ townships — 25:51's warner-everywhere). Gathered assembling; responses questioning; knowledge disclaiming. Messengers witnessing; God knowing; unseen His.

**Lā ʿIlma Lanā: Prophetic Knowledge Disclaimed**

*Qālū lā ʿilma lanā* — "we have no knowledge": messengers *disclaiming* response-knowledge — and the disclaimer's humility needs honoring (prophets all-knowing-denied, Divine-omniscience confessed). *Disclaimed*: hearts'-realities (belief-sincere versus hypocrisy-masked — 63:1's lying-professions; v. 61's commuters-concealing), responses'-sincerity (obedience-genuine versus conformity-pressured), outcomes'-finality (deathbed-states — repentance-timing — 4:18's deathbed-barred). *Known-and-testified*: delivery-performed (*ballaghnā* — conveying-witnessed — *"have I conveyed?"*), warnings-issued (signs-shown, arguments-made), patience-practiced (rejection-endured — 46:35's messenger-patience). *ʿAllāmu'l-ghuyūb* (Knower-of-unseens — intensive-plural: *all*-unseen — 9:78; 34:48; 59:22): omniscience confessed (hearts-known — 67:13–14; secrets-known — 20:7; futures-known — 31:34). Disclaim creaturely (knowledge-bounded); confess Divinely (unseen-His); witness faithfully (delivery-yours). Humility honoring; omniscience confessing; audit perfecting.

**ʿAllām al-Ghuyūb: Knower of All Unseen**

*Innaka Anta ʿAllāmu'l-ghuyūb* — "Thou art Knower-of-unseens": *ʿallām* (intensive-knower — knowing-thoroughly-perpetually) *al-ghuyūb* (the-unseens — plural: all-hidden) — omniscience superlatived — and the superlative needs its inventory (unseen-categories). *Hearts'-unseen*: intentions (Bukhārī 1 — deeds-intentioned), beliefs (sincerity-versus-hypocrisy — v. 61's concealments), motives (worship-pure versus show-tainted — 107:4–6). *Futures'-unseen*: Hour (31:34's five-keys — rain, wombs, earnings, death-place, Hour), outcomes (missions'-fruits — conversions-timing), destinies (Garden-or-Fire — 22:17's judging). *Pasts'-unseen*: histories (narratives-revealed — 11:49's Noah-unknown; 12:3's Joseph-best), secrets (conspiracies — 58:7's three-private; plots — 9:74's Tabūk-ambush). *Anta* (Thou — exclusivity: *Thou*-alone — messengers-disclaiming, God-knowing). Confess exclusively; trust totally; judged perfectly. *ʿAllām* superlativing; *ghuyūb* encompassing; messengers deferring.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:110

> **When God says, "O Jesus son of Mary! Remember My blessing upon thee and upon thy mother, when I aided thee with the Holy Spirit. Thou didst speak to people in the cradle and in maturity. And when I taught thee the Book, wisdom, the Torah, and the Gospel. And when thou didst create from clay the shape of a bird, by My leave, and thou didst breathe into it, and it became a bird, by My leave. And thou didst heal the blind and the leper, by My leave. And when thou didst raise the dead, by My leave. And when I restrained the Children of Israel from thee, when thou didst come unto them with clear proofs, but those who disbelieved among them said, 'This is naught but manifest sorcery.'"**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Remember My Blessing: Favors Catalogued to Christ**

*Remember* (*udhkur* — recall-commanded) *My blessing upon-thee-and-mother* (*niʿmatī ʿalayka wa ʿalā wālidatik*): favors *catalogued* (vv. 110's sevenfold: Spirit-aided, cradle-speaking, Book-taught, clay-birding, blind-leper-healing, dead-raising, Israel-restrained) — grace inventoried (blessings counted — 14:34's uncountable; 16:18). *Holy-Spirit-aided* (*ayyadtuka bi-Rūḥi'l-Qudus* — Gabriel-supported — 2:87, 253; 16:102): empowerment-Divine (miracles Spirit-enabled). *Cradle-and-maturity speaking* (*fi'l-mahdi wa kahlan* — 3:46; 19:29–33: cradle-defense — *"I am God's servant"* — maturity-preaching): speech-miraculous (infancy-eloquent, manhood-prophetic). *Book-wisdom-Torah-Gospel taught* (*ʿallamtuka* — Divine-tutored): knowledge-granted (scriptures-mastered — *kitāb*-writing, *ḥikmah*-wisdom, Torah-Gospel). Remember gratefully; favors sevenfold; blessings inventoried.

**Bi-Idhnī: Fourfold "By My Leave"**

*Bi-idhnī* (by-My-leave) — *fourfold*-repeated (birding, breathing, healing, raising): permission *insisting* (miracles Divinely-licensed, never independently-powered) — christology's safeguard (v. 17's worship-refuted; vv. 72–76's divinity-denied). *Clay-bird* (*takhluqu mina'ṭ-ṭīni ka-hay'ati'ṭ-ṭayr... fa-tanfukhu fīhā fa-takūnu ṭayran* — 3:49's parallel): creation-mimetic (God-like forming — Adam-parallel — 15:29's breathing; 3:59's likeness), life-conferred (*tanfukhu* — breathing — Divine-prerogative exercised-by-leave). *Blind-leper healed* (*tubri'u...*): incurables-cured (blindness leprosy — medicine-defying — 3:49). *Dead raised* (*tukhriju'l-mawtā* — bringing-forth — Lazarus-tradition; 3:49): resurrection-anticipated (eschatological-power previewed). *Bi-idhnī* ×4: leave-insisted (power borrowed), divinity-denied (leave-needing), gratitude-commanded (*udhkur* — remember-whose-leave). Miracles licensed; power borrowed; leave fourfold. *Bi-idhnī* safeguarding; christology bounding; worship Godward.

**"Manifest Sorcery": Clear Proofs Dismissed**

*Wa idh kafaftu Banī Isrā'īla ʿanka idh ji'tahum bi'l-bayyināt fa-qāla'lladhīna kafarū minhum in hādhā illā siḥrun mubīn* — "Israel-restrained when clear-proofs-brought, disbelievers-crying sorcery": protection-granted (assassination-foiled — 4:157's killing-denied), proofs-dismissed (*siḥr*-charged — stock-accusation: Moses — 10:77–78; Ṣāliḥ — 54:34–35; Muḥammad ﷺ — 74:24; 10:2). *Kafaftu* (I-restrained — Divine-protection: plots-foiled — crucifixion-averted — 4:157–158's raising; accusers-confused). *Bayyināt* (clear-proofs: miracles-manifest — birding-healing-raising — Spirit-evidenced), *siḥrun mubīn* (manifest-sorcery: clarity-conceded (*mubīn* — manifest!), source-denied (*siḥr* — sorcery!) — self-contradicting: manifest-yet-dismissed). Protected Divinely; proofs clearly; dismissed perversely. *Kafaftu* shielding; *bayyināt* shining; *siḥr*-cry exposing-deniers.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:111

> **And when I inspired the disciples, "Believe in Me and in My Messenger," they said, "We believe, and bear witness that we are submitted."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Disciples Inspired: The Ḥawāriyyūn's Confession**

*When I inspired* (*awḥaytu* — revelation-inspired: divinely-cast — dreams, strengthened-hearts, Jesus'-preaching-confirmed) *the disciples* (*ḥawāriyyūn* — helpers, purifiers — from *ḥ-w-r*: whiteness, purity — Jesus'-twelve): *"believe in-Me-and-My-Messenger"* (*bi-... wa bi-Rasūlī* — dual-belief: God-and-Jesus) — *they said: we-believe, witness our-submission* (*muslimūn* — submitted — cf. 3:52–53's identical-confession). The verse honors discipleship: inspiration-received (hearts-opened — v. 83's weepers-pattern), belief-declared (confession-public), witness-requested (*ishhad* — enrollment-sought — cf. v. 83's *uktubnā*; 3:53). *Ḥawāriyyūn* (helpers): Jesus'-aides (preaching-assisted, Table-requested — v. 112, witness-borne). Inspired Divinely; believing dually; witnessing requested. Disciples honored; submission confessed; enrollment sought.

**Awḥaytu: Inspiration to Non-Prophets**

*Wa idh awḥaytu ilā'l-ḥawāriyyīn* — "when I inspired disciples": *waḥy* (inspiration, revelation) to *non-prophets* — and non-prophetic-*waḥy* needs its taxonomy (revelation-grades). *Prophetic-waḥy*: law-bearing (Qur'an — 42:51's speaking-modes: inspiration, veil, messenger-sent), messenger-defining (prophets *receiving*-legislation). *Non-prophetic-waḥy*: maternal (Moses'-mother — 28:7: *"We inspired: suckle, fear-not"* — guidance-cast), apostolic (disciples — this verse: belief-inspired), natural (bees — 16:68: *"Lord inspired bees"* — instinct-programmed; heavens-earth — 41:12; 99:5: command-received). *Awḥaytu*-to-disciples: hearts-strengthened (belief-cast — certainty-gifted), preaching-confirmed (Jesus'-words seconded — 61:14's helping-God), mission-commissioned (Gospel-spreading — witness-bearing). *An āminū bī wa bi-Rasūlī* (believe-in-Me-and-Messenger): inspiration's *content* (dual-belief — God-and-Christ), confession's *trigger* (inspired → declaring). Inspire mercifully; believe dually; confess publicly. *Waḥy* graded; disciples strengthened; submission witnessed.

**Muslimūn: Disciples as "Submitted"**

*Qālū āmannā wa'shhad bi-annā muslimūn* — "we believe, witness we-are-submitted": disciples self-describing *muslimūn* (submitted-ones) — and the self-description's ecumenism needs celebrating (3:52's identical: *naḥnu anṣāru'Llāh... ishhad bi-annā muslimūn*). *Muslimūn*-pre-Muḥammad: Abrahamic (2:131–133: Abraham-Ismail-Isaac-Jacob — *muslimūn*), Josephite (12:101: *"take me musliman"*), Mosaic-magicians (7:126: *"take us muslimīn"*), Solomonic-Queen (27:44: Bilqīs — *"aslamtu"*), disciples (this verse; 3:52). *Islām*-universal (submission-perennial — 3:19's *dīn*; 3:85): prophets preaching-one (submission — 21:25; 42:13), communities responding-variously (laws differing — v. 48's *shirʿah* — submission uniting). *Ishhad* (witness! — imperative: enrollment-requested — cf. v. 83's *faktubnā*): confession *crowned* (belief declared, witness sought). Submitted universally; disciples particularly; witness requested. *Muslimūn* transcending-eras; *islām* perennial; confession crowning.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:112

> **When the disciples said, "O Jesus son of Mary! Is thy Lord able to send down upon us a table from heaven?" He said, "Reverence God, if you are believers."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**"Is Thy Lord Able?": The Table Request**

*When disciples said: O-Jesus-son-of-Mary! Is-thy-Lord-able* (*hal yastaṭīʿu Rabbuka* — can-ness questioned — power-or-willingness queried — readings varying: *yastaṭīʿu* (is-able) / *tastaṭīʿu* (can-you-ask?) — Ṭ, Z) *to send-down a table* (*mā'idah* — food-spread, feast — the sūrah's namesake!) *from-heaven*? *He said: revere-God if-believers* (*ittaqū'Llāha in kuntum mu'minīn* — cf. v. 57's *ittaqū... in kuntum*) — request rebuked-gently (sign-demanding faith-questioned — certainty-sufficient — miracles-believed). *Mā'idah* (table-spread — sūrah-titling — vv. 112–115's feast): provision-miracle (manna-parallel — 2:57; 7:160; 20:80), certainty-seeking (v. 113's hearts-assured — Abraham-pattern — 2:260's *"to assure heart"*). Asked doubtingly; rebuked gently; table pending. Sūrah namesaked; request recorded; reverence demanded.

**Hal Yastaṭīʿ: Ability Questioned, Faith Tested**

*Hal yastaṭīʿu Rabbuka an yunazzila ʿalaynā mā'idatan mina's-samā'* — "can thy Lord send-down a table?": *istiṭāʿah*-questioned (ability queried) — faith *tested* — and the question's readings need weighing (exegesis divided). *Reading one* (*yastaṭīʿu Rabbuka* — is-thy-Lord-able?): power-questioned (Divine-ability doubted — disciples' faith-imperfect — Jesus'-rebuke merited: *ittaqū* — believers doubt-not-power). *Reading two* (*tastaṭīʿu... an tas'ala Rabbaka* — can-you ask-thy-Lord?): asking-ability queried (Jesus'-intercession sought — *"can you request?"* — faith-intact — request-procedural). *The rebuke* (*ittaqū'Llāha in kuntum mu'minīn*): reverence-demanded (sign-seeking faith-questioning — believers content-with-proofs-given), belief-conditioned (*in kuntum* — if-truly — certainty-sufficient — v. 113's assurance-sought). *Parallels*: Abraham's birds (2:260 — assurance-*not*-doubt — *"to assure heart"*), Moses' vision-request (7:143 — longing-*not*-doubting), Israel's meat-craving (2:61 — appetite-complaining). Question readings; faith testing; rebuke gentle. Ability undoubted-ultimately; assurance sought-legitimately (v. 113); table granted-graciously (v. 115).

**Ittaqū in Kuntum: Reverence Conditioning Belief**

*Qāla'ttaqū'Llāha in kuntum mu'minīn* — "revere God, if you are believers": Jesus' *rebuke* — reverence conditioned-on-belief (*in kuntum* — if-truly) — and the conditional-rebuke recurs (v. 57's *ittaqū... in kuntum mu'minīn* — mockery-context; v. 112's sign-seeking-context). *The rebuke's gentleness*: *ittaqū* (revere! — positive-commanding, not wrath-pouring — *qāla* simply-saying), belief-assumed (*in kuntum* — if-you-are — discipleship-honored — v. 111's *muslimūn*). *The rebuke's logic*: believers sign-content (proofs-sufficient — birding-healing-raising witnessed — v. 110!), sign-seeking faith-questioning (certainty-doubted — assurance-elsewhere-sought). *The disciples' vindication* (v. 113): motives-clarified (eating, hearts-assured, truth-known, witness-borne — legitimate-ends!), request-persisted (rebuke-absorbed, table-sought). *In kuntum mu'minīn* (if-believers): conditional challenging (belief-proven — reverence-practiced), never belief-voiding (disciples honored — v. 111's inspiration). Rebuke gently; believe truly; revere consequently. Conditional challenging; discipleship honoring; table forthcoming.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:113

> **They said, "We desire to eat from it, and that our hearts may be assured, and that we may know that thou hast spoken truthfully to us, and that we may be among the witnesses thereof."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Four Motives: Eating, Assurance, Knowledge, Witness**

*We desire to eat from it* (*na'kulu minhā* — partaking: provision-shared — hunger-and-fellowship), *hearts-assured* (*taṭma'inna qulūbunā* — tranquility — cf. Abraham's *li-yaṭma'inna qalbī* — 2:260; 13:28's hearts-remembrance-assured), *knowing truth-spoken* (*naʿlama an qad ṣadaqtanā* — veracity-confirmed — Jesus'-truthfulness evidenced), *witnesses thereof* (*nakūna ʿalayhā mina'sh-shāhidīn* — testifying: Table-witnessed — generations-told). Four motives ascending: bodily (eating — need), emotional (assurance — peace), epistemic (knowledge — certainty), missional (witness — testimony). The motives vindicate (legitimate-ends — rebuke-absorbed — v. 112's *ittaqū* honored-through-clarification), request purified (doubt-cleansed — assurance-sought-Abrahamically). Eat needing; assured tranquil; knowing certainly; witnessing perpetually. Fourfold ascending; request vindicated; Table deserved.

**Taṭma'inna Qulūbunā: Hearts Seeking Assurance**

*Wa taṭma'inna qulūbunā* — "hearts assured": *iṭmi'nān* (tranquility, settled-certainty) *sought* — and assurance-seeking's legitimacy needs Abraham's precedent (2:260: *"show me how Thou givest life... to assure my heart"* — *li-yaṭma'inna qalbī* — faith-intact, assurance-sought). *Iṭmi'nān*-spectrum: remembrance-assured (13:28: *"hearts assured by God's remembrance"* — dhikr-tranquilizing), sight-assured (Abraham's birds — 2:260; disciples' Table — this verse — miracles-settling), promise-assured (8:10's *li-taṭma'inna bihī qulūbukum* — Badr-angels — victory-calming), faith-assured (16:106's *qalbuhū muṭma'innun bi'l-īmān* — coercion-withstanding — heart-settled). *Disciples' assurance*: Table-witnessed (provision-miraculous — certainty-settling), truth-confirmed (*ṣadaqtanā*-known — Jesus-verified), witness-enabled (testimony-grounded — generations-convinced). Seeking-assurance faithful (Abraham-precedented — doubt-absent, certainty-deepened), demanding-signs defiant (Thamūd-pattern — v. 102's asked-then-denied). Assured Abrahamically; settled miraculously; witnessing consequently. *Iṭmi'nān* legitimate; hearts tranquil; Table witnessing.

**Shāhidīn ʿAlayhā: Witnesses to the Table**

*Wa nakūna ʿalayhā mina'sh-shāhidīn* — "witnesses thereof": Table-*witnessing* — testimony-missional — and witnessing's perpetuity needs noting (vv. 112–115 *narrated* — Qur'an testifying — generations witnessing-through-text). *The witnesses*: disciples (Table-partaking — firsthand-testifying), Israel (Table-hearing — sign-reported), ummah (Table-reading — vv. 112–115 recited — witnessing-through-revelation). *ʿAlayhā* (thereof — *upon*-it: witnessing *over*-Table — testimony presiding, evidence-establishing). *Mina'sh-shāhidīn* (among-witnesses — cf. v. 83's *faktubnā maʿa'sh-shāhidīn*; v. 111's *ishhad*; 3:53): witness-rank sought (testimony-enrolled — truth-borne). *The Table's witness-function*: Jesus-verified (*ṣadaqtanā* — truth-known), God-glorified (provision-miraculous — power-displayed), deniers-warned (v. 115's unprecedented-punishment — Table-defied). Witness firsthand (disciples-partaking), witness textually (ummah-reciting), witness perpetually (generations-told). *Shāhidīn* enrolled; Table testifying; truth witnessed.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:114

> **Jesus son of Mary said, "O God, our Lord! Send down upon us a table from heaven, to be a festival for us — for the first of us and the last of us — and a sign from Thee. And provide for us, for Thou art the best of providers."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**ʿĪd for First and Last: The Festival Request**

*Jesus said: O-God-our-Lord* (*Allāhumma Rabbanā* — dual-vocative: *Allāhumma* — O-God! — 3:26; 39:46; *Rabbanā* — our-Lord! — intimacy-doubled) — *send-down a Table* (*anzil ʿalaynā mā'idatan* — supplication-voiced — request-interceded): *festival for-us* (*ʿīdan lanā* — recurring-celebration — feast-memorialized), *first-and-last* (*li-awwalinā wa ākhirinā* — generations-spanning — disciples-and-descendants feasting-perpetually), *sign-from-Thee* (*āyatan minka* — miracle-attested — Divine-authorship evidenced). *ʿĪd* (festival — recurring-joy — from *ʿ-w-d*: returning — celebration-cycling): Table-festive (provision-celebrated — gratitude-ritualized), generations-uniting (first-last feasting — continuity-embodied). Provide (*urzuqnā* — supplication-doubled — Table-and-sustenance), *best-of-providers* (*khayru'r-rāziqīn* — 22:58; 23:72; 62:11 — Provider-superlatived). Supplicate dually; festival perpetually; provide bestly. *ʿĪd* requested; generations spanned; sign granted-pending.

**Allāhumma Rabbanā: The Dual Vocative**

*Allāhumma Rabbanā anzil...* — "O-God, our-Lord, send-down": the *dual-vocative* — *Allāhumma* (O-God — *Allāh* + *-umma* — supplication's grand-address — 3:26's sovereignty; 8:32's challenge; 39:46's judgment) + *Rabbanā* (our-Lord — intimacy-possessive — *"our"* — relationship-claimed). *The combination's force*: transcendence-and-intimacy (God-grand *and* Lord-ours — majesty-and-nearness), urgency-and-confidence (*anzil* imperative — send! — intercession-bold; *khayru'r-rāziqīn* — best-Provider — confidence-grounded). *Qur'anic Allāhumma-sites*: 3:26 (sovereignty-hymned), 5:114 (Table-requested), 8:32 (pagans-challenging — *"rain stones"* — vocative-mocked), 10:10 (Garden-prayer — *"glory"* — vocative-blissed), 39:46 (judgment-invoked). *Rabbanā*-supplications: ubiquitous (2:127–128, 201, 285–286; 3:8, 16, 53, 191–194 — believers' breath). Invoke grandly (*Allāhumma*); claim intimately (*Rabbanā*); request boldly (*anzil*). Dual-vocative voicing; Table descending; festival coming.

**Khayr al-Rāziqīn: Best of Providers**

*Warzuqnā wa Anta khayru'r-rāziqīn* — "provide us — Thou art best-of-providers": *khayrur-rāziqīn* (Providers'-Best — 22:58; 23:72; 34:39; 62:11) — provision's superlative — and the superlative needs its providers-comparison (all-providing weighed, God-best). *Human-providers*: employers (wages-paying — provision-mediated), parents (children-feeding — provision-channeled), hosts (guests-serving — provision-shared) — provision *distributed* (channels-flowing, sources-secondary). *Divine-providing*: creating-provision (food-grown — 36:33–35; 80:24–32), delivering-unasked (wombs-fed — 31:34; beasts-sustained — 11:6; 29:60), providing-enemies (deniers-fed — 17:20 — generosity-undiscriminating), bestowing-gratefully (thanks-multiplying — 14:7's increase). *Khayru* (best — superlative: generosity-greatest, reliability-surest, bounty-widest — 51:58's *Razzāq* forceful-intensive). Request provision (*urzuqnā* — Table-and-beyond); confess Best (*khayru* — comparison-concluded); receive gratefully (festival-thanking). Providers compared; Best confessed; Table granted.

---
## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:115

> **God said, "Truly I shall send it down upon you. But whosoever among you disbelieves thereafter, I shall punish him with a punishment wherewith I have punished no one among the worlds."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Table Granted — With History's Sternest Warning**

*God said: I shall send it down* (*munazziluhā* — emphatic: sending-certain) — *but disbelieving-after* (*man yakfur baʿda* — Table-witnessed then-denied) — *punished unprecedentedly* (*ʿadhāban lā uʿadhdhibu aḥadan mina'l-ʿālamīn* — torment unmatched-worldwide). The grant-and-threat: Table-descending (vv. 112–114's request answered — feast-granted, festival-established — v. 114's *ʿīd*), disbelief-threatened (sign-magnitude matching punishment-magnitude — proofs-greatest → denials-gravest). *Lā uʿadhdhibu aḥadan* (punishing-none-like): unprecedented-torment (Table-deniers uniquely-punished — sign-seen feasted-then-denied — ingratitude-supreme). The reports: Table-descending (bread-and-meat, or all-foods — Ṭ, W; thousands-feasting), some-hoarding (provision-stored — command-violated — *maskh*-traditions: apes-and-swine — v. 60's *qiradah-khanāzīr*), deniers-punished (threat-executed). Granted graciously; threatened sternly; feasted-or-punished. Table descending; warning history-sternest; ingratitude unmatched-punished.

**Munazziluhā: The Grant's Emphasis**

*Qāla'Llāhu innī munazziluhā ʿalaykum* — "I-am sending-it-down": *munazzil* (active-participle — sending-in-progress — certainty-imminent) + *innī* (truly-I — emphasis-doubled) — grant *certain* (request-granted — Jesus'-intercession accepted — v. 114's *Allāhumma-Rabbanā* answered). *The Table's descriptions* (tradition): heavenly-spread (bread-meat-fish — varieties — Ṭ, W), thousands-feeding (multitudes-satisfied — morsels-multiplying — manna-parallel — 2:57), festival-establishing (*ʿīdan* — v. 114 — commemoration-cycling). *The Table's lessons*: supplication-answered (prophets'-requests granted — Abraham's birds — 2:260; Moses' water — 2:60; Jesus' Table), provision-miraculous (God-feeding — *khayru'r-rāziqīn* — v. 114 — Best-providing), gratitude-demanded (feast-thanked — ingratitude-threatened — this verse's *man yakfur*). Sending certainly (*innī-munazziluhā*); feasting communally; thanking obligatorily. Grant emphasized; intercession accepted; Table descending.

**Unprecedented Punishment: Signs Proportioned to Sentences**

*Fa-man yakfur baʿdu minki fa-innī uʿadhdhibuhū ʿadhāban lā uʿadhdhibuhū aḥadan mina'l-ʿālamīn* — "disbelieving-after: punishing unprecedentedly": punishment *proportioned* to sign-magnitude — proofs-greatest → sentences-gravest — and the proportionality needs its principle (revelation's justice-equation). *The equation*: knowledge-aggravating (signs-witnessed — denial-deliberate — 27:14's certainty-with-injustice), bounty-aggravating (Tables-feasted — ingratitude-supreme — 14:7's increase-or-severe), clarity-aggravating (*bayyināt*-manifest — dismissal-perverse — v. 110's *siḥr*-cry). *Unprecedented-cases*: Table-deniers (this verse — torment-unmatched), Pharaoh's-host (10:92's body-preserved — sign-made — drowning-witnessed), Quraysh-warned (17:59's signs-withheld — denial-forestalling-mercy — *"We withhold signs, former-deniers"*). *Lā uʿadhdhibu aḥadan mina'l-ʿālamīn* (none-worldwide-like): superlative-sentencing (torment's maximum — ingratitude's wage). Witnessed greatly; denied gravely; punished unprecedentedly. Proportioned justly; threatened sternly; ingratitude maximally-punished.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:116

> **And when God says, "O Jesus son of Mary! Didst thou say unto mankind, 'Take me and my mother as two gods besides God?'" He will say, "Glory be unto Thee! It is not for me to say that to which I have no right. If I had said it, Thou wouldst know it. Thou knowest what is within me, and I know not what is within Thee. Truly it is Thou Who art the Knower of things unseen."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**"Didst Thou Say?": Christ Interrogated**

*When God says* (*wa idh qāla* — eschatological-questioning — cf. v. 109's messengers-gathered): *O-Jesus-son-of-Mary! Did-you-say* (*a-anta qulta* — thou-saying? — interrogative-establishing): *"take-me-and-mother as-two-gods besides-God"* (*ittakhidhūnī wa ummiya ilāhayni min dūni'Llāh* — dual-deification: Jesus-and-Mary godded)? *He will say: Glory-be-Thee* (*subḥānaka* — transcendence-declared — deification-refuted-by-glorification)! *Not-for-me saying-unrightful* (*mā yakūnu lī an aqūla mā laysa lī bi-ḥaqq* — right-denied — prophet-bounded — creature-confessed). *Had-I-said, Thou-knowing* (*in kuntu qultuhū fa-qad ʿalimtah* — omniscience-conceded — concealment-impossible). *Thou-knowest within-me, I-know-not within-Thee* (*taʿlamu mā fī nafsī wa-lā aʿlamu mā fī nafsik* — knowledge-asymmetric — Creator-knowing-creature, creature-uncomprehending-Creator). *Thou Knower-unseen* (*ʿAllāmu'l-ghuyūb* — v. 109's confessed — repeated). Interrogated publicly; glorifying instantly; disclaiming totally. Question establishing; answer exonerating; worship Godward.

**Subḥānaka: Glorification as Refutation**

*Qāla subḥānaka* — "Glory be to Thee!": *tasbīḥ* (glorification — transcendence-declaring) *as-refutation* — deification-charges answered-by-transcendence — and the tasbīḥ-refutation needs its logic (glorifying-God *un*-gods-all-else). *The logic*: God-glorified (transcendent — partners-impossible — 112:1–4; 2:255), Jesus-creatured (worshipper — glorification-*offering*, not receiving), Mary-sainted (worshipper — v. 75's *ṣiddīqah*-eating — glorification-needing). *Tasbīḥ*-as-first-response: angels-glorifying (2:30's *"we glorify"* — deputation-declining), prophets-glorifying (Jonah's *"glory-Thee, I-wronged"* — 21:87; Moses' *"glory-Thee, I-turn"* — 7:143), Jesus-glorifying (this verse — deification-refuting). *Mā yakūnu lī* (not-for-me — being-impossible: prophet-*cannot* claim-divinity — office-bounding — 3:79's *"no-man given-Book... saying: worship-me"*). Refute-by-glorifying (transcendence-declaring), disclaim-by-bounding (office-honoring), exonerated-thereby (charges-voided). *Subḥānaka* first-wording; refutation completing; worship Godward-only.

**Asymmetric Knowledge: Thou Knowest Me, I Know Not Thee**

*Taʿlamu mā fī nafsī wa-lā aʿlamu mā fī nafsik* — "Thou knowest within-me, I know-not within-Thee": knowledge *asymmetric* — Creator-comprehending-creature, creature-uncomprehending-Creator — and the asymmetry grounds all theology (God-known-through-revelation, never comprehended — 6:103's sights-ungrasping; 20:110's knowledge-unencompassing). *Thou-knowest-me* (*taʿlamu mā fī nafsī* — within-ness known: thoughts-known — 50:16's whisper; motives-known — intentions-weighed; utterances-known — *in kuntu qultuhū ʿalimtah* — had-said known). *I-know-not-Thee* (*lā aʿlamu mā fī nafsik* — within-ness unknown: Essence-uncomprehended — *lā yuḥīṭūn* — 20:110; decrees-unfathomed — wisdom-trusted; timing-unknown — Hour-hidden — 7:187). *Innaka Anta ʿAllāmu'l-ghuyūb* (Thou Knower-unseens — v. 109's messengers-confessing — Jesus-joining): omniscience confessed-corporately (all-prophets disclaiming, God-alone knowing). Asymmetry confessing; omniscience conceding; exoneration completing. Known-totally; knowing-partially; glorifying-perpetually.

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:117

> **"I said naught to them save what Thou didst command me: 'Worship God, my Lord and your Lord.' And I was a witness over them, so long as I remained among them. But when Thou didst take me up, Thou hast been the Watcher over them. And Thou art Witness over all things."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**"Worship God, My Lord and Yours": Christ's True Message**

*I-said-naught save commanded* (*mā qultu lahum illā mā amartanī bih* — message-bounded — prophetic-fidelity): *"worship-God, my-Lord-and-yours"* (*uʿbudū'Llāha Rabbī wa Rabbakum* — cf. 3:51; 19:36; 43:64 — Jesus'-tawḥīd identical-across-sūrahs; 5:72's worship-commanded). *My-Lord-and-yours* (*Rabbī-wa-Rabbakum* — Lord-*shared*: Jesus-...lorded — worshipper-confessed — divinity-impossible — the lorded lord-no). *Witness among-them* (*shahīdan ʿalayhim mā dumtu fīhim* — presence-witnessing — ministry-observing — disciples-guided, deniers-warned), *taken-up* (*tawaffaytanī* — death-or-raising — 3:55; 4:157–158 — ministry-ending), *Watcher-after* (*Anta'r-Raqību ʿalayhim* — God-watching — guidance-continuing — accountability-preserving). *Witness-all* (*Shahīdun ʿalā kulli shay'* — omniscience-testifying). Said commandedly; lorded sharedly; witnessed presently. Message tawḥīd; ministry bounded; Watcher perpetual.

**Mā Qultu Illā: Prophetic Fidelity Bounded**

*Mā qultu lahum illā mā amartanī bihī* — "said-naught save commanded": prophetic-*fidelity* — message *bounded*-by-command — and the boundedness defines prophethood (messengers transmitting, never inventing — 69:44–46's aorta-threat for forgery; 10:15's *"not-for-me changing"*; 53:3–4's desire-unspoken). *Commanded-content* (*mā amartanī* — worship-tawḥīd: *uʿbudū'Llāha* — worship-God; *Rabbī-wa-Rabbakum* — Lord-shared; 3:51; 19:36; 43:64 — Jesus'-message uniform). *Uncommanded-denied* (*mā qultu... illā* — exclusivity: deification-unsaid — Trinity-unpreached — goddess-Mary unproclaimed — v. 116's charges voided). *Fidelity-vindicated* (eschatologically-testified — this verse's courtroom — Jesus-exonerated, Church-claims unsubstantiated-by-Christ). *The lesson*: prophets *bounded*-messengers (commands-transmitting — additions-forbidden — 5:67's *balligh-all*), communities *bounded*-followers (prophets-obeyed — deifications-refused — 3:79). Bounded transmitting; bounded following; fidelity vindicating. *Mā-illā* excluding; commands including; courtroom exonerating.

**Raqīb After Rafʿ: The Watcher Perpetual**

*Fa-lammā tawaffaytanī kunta Anta'r-Raqība ʿalayhim wa Anta ʿalā kulli shay'in Shahīd* — "when Thou tookest-me, Thou-wast Watcher — Witness-all": *raqābah* (watching) *transferring* (Jesus-present → God-perpetual) — ministry-ending, vigilance-continuing. *Tawaffaytanī* (Thou-tookest-me — *tawaffī*: taking-fully — death-or-sleep-or-raising — 3:55's *mutawaffīka*; 4:157–158's raising; 39:42's souls-taken): ministry-terminating (presence-ending — witness-presence closing). *Anta'r-Raqīb* (Thou-the-Watcher — *raqīb*: vigilant-observer — 4:1; 33:52; 50:18): vigilance-continuing (absence-unwatched-never — communities monitored — deviations-recorded). *ʿAlā kulli shay'in Shahīd* (Witness-over-*all* — universality: Jesus'-community *and* all-communities — 4:41's Prophet-witnessing; 22:17's judging-between; v. 109's messengers-gathered). Taken-up ministry-ending; Watcher vigilance-continuing; Witness universality-testifying. *Raqīb* perpetual; *Shahīd* total; accountability unbroken.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:118

> **"If Thou punishest them, they are Thy servants. And if Thou forgivest them, truly Thou art the Mighty, the Wise."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**"They Are Thy Servants": Christ's Final Plea**

*If-Thou-punishest* (*in tuʿadhdhibhum* — punishment-acknowledged — justice-conceded): *they-are-Thy-servants* (*ʿibāduk* — servants-Thine — ownership-confessed — punishment-rightful — Master-disposing). *If-Thou-forgivest* (*in taghfir lahum* — forgiveness-pleaded — mercy-hoped): *Thou-art Mighty-Wise* (*ʿAzīz Ḥakīm* — Might-unconstrained — forgiveness-unchallenged; Wisdom-perfect — forgiveness-well-placed). The plea's perfection: justice-conceded (punishment *Thy-right* — servants Thine), mercy-pleaded (forgiveness *Thy-praise* — Mighty-Wise Thou), outcome-deferred (punish-or-forgive — *in-in* — conditionals-both — will-Thine). The Prophet ﷺ wept-reciting (night-praying — *"my ummah, my ummah"* — Muslim 202 — intercession-pleading — Jesus'-words adopted). Plead concedingly; hope mightily; defer totally. Servants owned; Mighty-Wise praised; outcome Divine.

**In Tuʿadhdhib/In Taghfir: The Double Conditional**

*In tuʿadhdhibhum fa-innahum ʿibāduk, wa-in taghfir lahum fa-innaka Anta'l-ʿAzīzu'l-Ḥakīm* — "punish-servants; forgive-Mighty-Wise": the *double*-conditional (*in... wa-in* — if-punish, if-forgive) — outcomes *both*-Divine — and the doubling's diplomacy needs admiring (intercession's perfect-form). *Punish-conditional* (*in tuʿadhdhibhum*): justice-conceded (punishment-unprotested — servants-owned — rights-acknowledged), no-intercession-against-justice (wrongs-unexcused — deification-grave — vv. 72–73's *kafara*). *Forgive-conditional* (*in taghfir*): mercy-pleaded (forgiveness-hoped — intercession-offered — Jesus-pleading followers-erring), Names-grounded (*ʿAzīz-Ḥakīm* — Might-forgiving-unchallenged — none-constraining; Wisdom-forgiving-well — placements-perfect). *The diplomacy*: neither demanding (forgiveness-uncompelled — *in*-conditional), nor despairing (punishment-unassumed — *in*-conditional), but deferring (will-Divine — wisdom-trusted). *The Prophet's ﷺ adoption* (Muslim 202 — weeping *"ummatī"* — intercession-modeled — Jesus'-plea echoing). Condition doubly; concede justly; plead hopefully. *In-in* deferring; *ʿibāduk* owning; *ʿAzīz-Ḥakīm* grounding.

**ʿAzīz-Ḥakīm: Might and Wisdom Grounding Forgiveness**

*Fa-innaka Anta'l-ʿAzīzu'l-Ḥakīm* — "Thou art the Mighty, Wise": forgiveness *grounded* — Might-and-Wisdom — not *Ghafūr-Raḥīm* (Forgiving-Merciful — expected!) — and the unexpected-Names need pondering (Jesus-pleading-forgiveness invoking-Might-Wisdom — why?). *Why-not-Ghafūr-Raḥīm*: deification-grave (*shirk* — 4:48's unforgiven-unrepented — mercy-presumption unfitting — intercession-humble), courtroom-solemn (eschatological-trial — verdicts-pending — Names-majestic). *Why-ʿAzīz* (Mighty — irresistible: forgiveness-unchallenged — none-vetoing — servants-His — *ʿibāduk*; punishment-inescapable — none-rescuing — Might-both). *Why-Ḥakīm* (Wise — placements-perfect: forgiving-whom-fitting — wisdom-discriminating — erring-sincere versus defiant-knowing; punishing-whom-deserving — 22:17's judging-between). *Anta* (Thou — exclusivity: *Thou*-alone Mighty-Wise — judgment-Thine). Ground forgivingly-in-Might (unchallenged), wisely-in-Wisdom (well-placed), exclusively-in-Thee (*Anta*). Unexpected-Names pondering; intercession perfecting; courtroom deferring.
