## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:119

> **God says, "This is the Day when the truthful shall benefit from their truthfulness. Theirs shall be Gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein forever. God is pleased with them, and they are pleased with Him. That is the great triumph."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**"This Is the Day": Truthfulness Profiting**

*God says* (*qāla'Llāhu* — Divine-verdict voicing — courtroom-concluding — vv. 109–118's interrogations-answered): *this-is-the-Day* (*hādhā yawmu* — demonstrative: *this*-Day — Resurrection-presented) *truthful-benefiting from-truthfulness* (*yanfaʿu'ṣ-ṣādiqīna ṣidquhum* — truth-profiting — sincerity-dividend — cf. 39:33's truthful-rewarded; 33:24's truthful-recompensed). *Gardens-rivers-abiding-forever* (*khālidīna fīhā abadan* — perpetuity-superlatived — *abadan*-forever — 4:122; 9:100; 64:9; 98:8): truth's-wage (sincerity Gardens-earning — vv. 85's confessors; 119's truthful). *Ṣidq* (truthfulness — word-and-deed aligned — 33:23's covenant-trued; 49:15's striving-believed): benefiting-Day (lies-exposed — 39:60's faces-blackened; truths-rewarded — faces-whitened — 3:106–107). Verdict voicing; Day presenting; truth profiting. *Hādhā-yawmu* demonstrating; *ṣidq* dividend-paying; Gardens perpetuity-granting.

**Yanfaʿu'ṣ-Ṣādiqīn: Truth's Dividend Day**

*Hādhā yawmu yanfaʿu'ṣ-ṣādiqīna ṣidquhum* — "Day truthful-benefit from-truthfulness": *nafʿ* (benefit, profit) *truth-sourced* — sincerity's dividend-Day — and the dividend needs its portfolio (truth-investments maturing). *Truth-invested*: confessions (v. 85's Christians — Gardens-for-saying; v. 111's disciples — *muslimūn*-declaring), covenant-keeping (33:23's *ṣadaqū* — pledge-truing — martyrs-fulfilling), striving (49:15's believing-doubtless-striving — wealth-selves spending), witness-bearing (v. 83's *shāhidīn*-requested; truth-testified). *Truth-dividends*: Gardens-rivers-forever (this verse — perpetuity), pleasure-mutual (*raḍiya... wa raḍū* — see below), triumph-great (*fawz*-superlatived — see below). *Lies'-bankruptcy*: faces-blackened (39:60's liars — *kadhabū*-branded), Fire-abiding (104:1's slanderers; 83:1's defrauders — untruth-punished), profitless-Day (26:88's wealth-children unavailing — lies-unavailing). Invest truthfully (confess-keep-strive-witness); dividend Day-coming; Gardens perpetuity-paying. *Yanfaʿu* profiting; *ṣidq* sourcing; Day demonstrating.

**Riḍwān and Fawz: Pleasure Mutual, Triumph Great**

*Raḍiya'Llāhu ʿanhum wa raḍū ʿanhu, dhālika'l-fawzu'l-ʿaẓīm* — "God-pleased with-them, they-pleased with-Him — that-is triumph-great": *riḍwān* (pleasure) *mutual* (Divine-and-human — 9:100; 58:22; 98:8 — believers'-crown), *fawz* (triumph) *great* (*ʿaẓīm* — superlatived — 4:13; 9:100; 44:57; 85:11). *Raḍiya ʿanhum* (God-pleased): acceptance-supreme (worship-received — deeds-rewarded — 89:28's *rāḍiyatan-marḍiyyah* — soul-pleased-pleasing), *riḍwān*-greatest (9:72's *riḍwānun akbar* — pleasure-greatest — Gardens-surpassing — Beatific-pleasure). *Raḍū ʿanhu* (they-pleased): contentment-complete (decrees-accepted — portions-sufficing — 89:28; 93:5's *sa-yurḍīk* — Lord-pleasing-Thee), grievances-none (justice-witnessed — mercy-received — 10:10's Garden-praise). *Fawz ʿaẓīm* (triumph-great): salvation-titled (Hell-escaped — 3:185's *fāz... zuḥziḥa* — Fire-removed; Gardens-entered — this verse), greatness-superlatived (*ʿaẓīm* — triumph-unmatched). Pleased mutually; triumph greatly; Day eternally. *Riḍwān* crowning; *fawz* titling; sūrah concluding-triumphantly.

---

## Sūrah al-Mā'idah 5:120

> **Unto God belongs sovereignty over the heavens and the earth and whatsoever is therein. And He is Powerful over all things.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Unto God Belongs Sovereignty: The Sūrah's Final Chord**

*Unto-God-belongs sovereignty* (*li'Llāhi mulku* — cf. v. 40's *li'Llāhi mulku*; v. 17's *li'Llāhi mulku*; v. 72's *li'Llāhi mulku* — *mulk*-refrain fourfold!) *over-heavens-and-earth-and-therein* (*wa mā fīhinna* — all-contained — universality-total): the sūrah's *final-chord* (covenant-to-sovereignty — v. 1's contracts → v. 120's Kingdom). *Mulk*-refrain (vv. 17, 40, 72, 120): christology-contexted (17, 72 — divinity-denied — sovereignty-God's), penalty-contexted (40 — punishment-forgiveness — sovereignty-owning), sūrah-sealing (120 — all-gathered — sovereignty-universal). *Wa mā fīhinna* (whatsoever-therein — contents-included: creatures-all — seen-unseen — heavens-earth-filling). *Powerful-all* (*ʿalā kulli shay'in Qadīr* — cf. v. 17, 19, 40 — *Qadīr*-refrain): omnipotence-sealing (sovereignty-empowered — Kingdom-enforced). Belongs Divinely; contains totally; seals powerfully. *Mulk* fourfold-refraining; sūrah sovereignty-concluding; Kingdom God's.

**Mulk Fourfold: The Sūrah's Sovereignty Refrain**

*Li'Llāhi mulku's-samāwāti wa'l-arḍ* — "God's is sovereignty": *fourfold*-refrained (vv. 17, 40, 72, 120) — the sūrah's *sovereignty-spine* — and the spine's placements need mapping (refrain-contexts). *Mulk-one* (v. 17 — christology: Messiah-divinity denied — sovereignty-God's — destroying-able). *Mulk-two* (v. 40 — penology: punishment-forgiveness willed — sovereignty-owning — wills-twin). *Mulk-three* (v. 72 — christology: Messiah-divinity denied — sovereignty-God's — creating-willed). *Mulk-four* (v. 120 — doxology: sūrah-sealed — sovereignty-universal — *wa-mā-fīhinna* — contents-all). *The spine's theology*: sovereignty *denying*-rivals (Christs un-godded — 17, 72), sovereignty *owning*-judgment (penalties-providenced — 40), sovereignty *sealing*-covenants (contracts-crowned — 120 — v. 1's *ʿuqūd* → v. 120's *mulk*: obligations-unto-Owner). *Qadīr*-paired (Powerful-all — 17, 19, 40, 120): sovereignty *empowered* (Kingdom-enforced — wills-executed). Refrain fourfold; spine sovereign; sūrah sealed. *Mulk* denying-owning-sealing; *Qadīr* empowering; covenants crowned.

**ʿAlā Kulli Shay'in Qadīr: Omnipotence Sealing All**

*Wa Huwa ʿalā kulli shay'in Qadīr* — "He is over-all Powerful": *Qadīr* (All-Powerful — *qudrah*-perfect — 2:20, 106, 109, 284; 3:26, 29 — Qur'an's omnipotence-refrain) *sealing* the sūrah (final-words — covenant-Code concluding-in-capability). *ʿAlā-kulli-shay'* (over-*all*-things: universality — Tables-sent (v. 115 — provision-powered), Messiahs-created (v. 110, 72 — virgins-impregnated — creation-willed), dead-raised (v. 110 — graves-emptied — resurrection-previewed), deniers-punished (v. 115 — unprecedented-tormented), truthful-rewarded (v. 119 — Gardens-granted), all-gathered (vv. 109, 119 — assemblies-convened). *Huwa* (He — exclusivity: *He*-alone — partners-denied — vv. 72–76; rivals-voided — vv. 17, 116–117). *The seal's fitness*: sūrah-of-covenants (contracts-commanded — v. 1) sealed-by-sovereignty (Owner-declared — v. 120) empowered-by-omnipotence (*Qadīr* — enforcement-guaranteed). Powerful totally; sealing fittingly; sūrah concluding. *Qadīr* omnipotent; *mulk* owned; covenants crowned-eternally.
